# Project History

✅ **THR-466** (2026-06-29, Encounter Format Migration) — **Non-local-scale branching encounters complete (5 of 5).** Closed the content gap where all 23 authored branching encounters were `scale:'local'`. Batch 2 (3/5, this run) authored the three remaining regional encounters to the exemplar quality bar: **The Page Beneath the Saint** (veil — Seer↔Manipulator: an archivist's buried truth that could unmake a regional faith — bury it deeper vs. let the truth surface), **The Verdict That Burns** (eye — Witness↔Judge: a magistrate whose just verdict burns the region — deliver it vs. witness and withhold the hammer), and **The Granaries in the Famine Year** (gold — Patron↔Extractor: a merchant-prince cornering a region's grain in a famine — press the granaries open vs. let the market close, a deliberate inversion where intervention is the mercy and restraint the bleeding). All `story_beat`/rarity-4 via the shared `withEncounterContract` builder, two god-action branches each (active intervention + the always-valid "let them handle it" restraint pole), full dual aftermath suites with cool failure at every branch, `// TODO(THR-498)` repeatability markers; registered in `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` + `LOCATION_BRANCHING_ENCOUNTER_TEMPLATES`. Batch 1 (2/5, prior run) shipped the cosmic Star + regional Stone encounters plus the engine pre-check that proved the firing path is scale-agnostic. 39 new tests this run; full suite green (777 files, 11891 tests; migration count 26→29, legendary-tier cap 12→16 for the marquee batch), vite build green, CLI smoke spawned both new regional encounters through a 30-tick run clean. Browser-verify N/A (content/data; UI pillar N/A — uses the shipped encounter surface).

✅ **THR-518** (2026-06-29, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Consecrate relic variant: mint a permanent relic + dynamic `upkeepArtifactId` injection.** Closed the THR-511 deferral. THR-511 shipped consecrate as a pure-data sustained-control card and `spawnControlEffect` already carried a *static* `upkeepArtifactId` through to the effect — but the relic variant needs the id to be *dynamic* (a freshly-minted node that doesn't exist until resolution). Added `ControlSpec.mintUpkeepRelic?: MintUpkeepRelicSpec` (`relicName` + optional `tags`); `spawnControlEffect` now, when that field is set and a graph is available, mints a permanent relic artifact (`lossCondition:'permanent'`), binds it to the establishing ascendant via a `possesses` edge, and sets the spawned effect's `upkeepArtifactId` to the **freshly-minted node id** (a minted id always overrides a static one). The existing `relic_upkeep_substitute` consumer (`getUpkeepStatus` / `phaseControlEffects`, THR-509) then waives `perTickCost` while the relic lives and lapses the effect (`upkeep_relic_destroyed`) on its destruction — the rival contestation vector — with zero new consumer wiring. **No new GraphOp, no resolution intercept** — the relic variant stays pure data: a second card `action.consecrate-relic` ("Enshrine Relic", `essenceCost: CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT = 13`, empty `perTickCost`, same faith-spread aura), granted alongside `action.consecrate` by `beat.pool.invest.the_hallowed_place` and bucketed `unlockable-generic`. Mint is deterministic (id + content from owner/target/tick, no PRNG), fail-soft (no graph → no mint, static id stands), and emits a `control_effect_relic_minted` trace. Three pillars: Engine (`MintUpkeepRelicSpec` type + the mint path in `spawnControlEffect`); Content (the new card + unlock-catalog/beat-grant wiring + `CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT`); UI N/A (the card renders through the existing data-driven ActionCard path — no UI-pillar file touched). 6 new tests (`controlEffectSpawn.test.ts`: mint+inject, deterministic id, minted overrides static, no-graph fail-soft, end-to-end waive-while-alive + lapse-on-destroy). Verification: full suite green, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` 0 net-new (3484 = 3484 baseline), 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 398 agents, 42 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt (types/engine/data only — existing ActionCard render path). PR pending.

✅ **THR-515** (2026-06-29, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Expand pool + buckets as the per-graph expression cards ship.** THR-505 grounded the unlock catalogue only in the cards shipping then; the §4.4 expression verbs `action.consecrate`/`bestow`/`anoint` (THR-511/512/513) had since landed but no pool beat granted them and they carried no `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` entry. Added 3 `unlockable-generic` bucket entries (consecrate/bestow/anoint) + 3 deeper-expression `investment` pool beats that grant them — `beat.pool.invest.the_hallowed_place`/`the_favored_soul`/`the_chosen_banner` — mirroring `raw_relic`'s treatment of `imbue` (one beat per per-graph verb), each backed by a Threadbare-voice content template in `ascendant-pool-beat-templates.ts` (pool 14→17; templates length-matched). Eligibility `unthreaded_target` (consistent with sibling investment beats; the card's own target filters — subtype/awareness/faction — gate when it actually surfaces in the hand). The reach-gated `invest.*` half of THR-515's scope stays blocked — **no such card templates exist yet** (verified: a search for `id: 'invest.` in `src/data/` is empty) — split to **THR-523**; the `reach-gated` bucket + `requiresReach` field already exist for when they land. Three pillars: Engine N/A (no engine file — buckets are authoring metadata; the reach gate reads `template.requiresReach` directly); Content (the 3 beats + 3 templates + 3 bucket entries); UI N/A (prose renders through the existing tested `AscendantBeatModal` path — the `enrichProse`-clean test covers the render contract). Verification: THR-505 (9) + THR-514 (8) coverage suites green; full suite **11821 green** (772 files); vite build green; `tsc -b` clean for both changed `src/data/` files. Browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file touched); engine-smoke N/A (no `src/engine`/tick file). PR pending.

✅ **THR-520** (2026-06-29, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Beat-driven graph seeding: auto-thread seat/artifact on spine-beat resolution.** Closed the THR-504 deferral: the THR-517 resolve contract only granted cards, so the throne/artifact the onboarding narrates appeared only when the player manually fired them. Added an additive `seedsGraph?: BeatGraphSeed` tag on `BeatDefinition` (`home_seat` | `threaded_artifact`); `resolvePendingBeat` now runs the seed on resolution and records `BeatRecord.seededNodeIds` (locked grant/spine tests untouched — they carry no tag). New `src/engine/ascendantBeatSeeding.ts`: `home_seat` resolves The First → its `located_at` settlement (climbs a sublocation to its parent), `setHomeSeat` begins `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT` income, falls back to setHomeSeat's deterministic default when no First; `threaded_artifact` mints a sphere-flavored artifact via THR-509 `pickSphereFlavoredEffect` (seeded `mulberry32(seed+tick)`), `thread`s it (ascendant→artifact), grants it to the bonded First via a `possesses` edge (skipped fail-soft when no First). Beat 0 seeds nothing (`MeetingEncounter` already threads The First — no double-thread). New registered `ascendant.beat.seeded` trace + `BeatSeededTrace`. Fail-soft throughout (no ascendant/location/sphere → empty seed + `failSoft` trace). Three pillars: Engine (seeding module + resolve wiring); Content (the two spine-beat tags); UI N/A (GameView resolve call unchanged — seeding is transparent; seat signifier/HUD read the now-set graph through existing surfaces). 16 new tests (`ascendantBeatSeeding.test.ts`). Verification: full suite **11821 green** (772 files), vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 398 agents, 42 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file touched). PR pending.

✅ **THR-519** (2026-06-28, Action System & Unlocks) — **Self-updating Action Catalog + wiki page.** New `scripts/generate-action-catalog.ts` imports the live `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` + `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` + starter helpers and emits `public/action-catalog.generated.json` — one entry per ascendant-facing template (164: action/divine/hex/loc+sub/artifact/threads, mirroring the Codex prefix grouping) with `id, name, spellName, reach, sphereAffinity, crudType, scale, rarityTier, intrinsicTier, apCost, essenceCost, durationMode, hasControlSpec, targetCategories, targetSubtypes, requiresReach, starter, beatGranted, bucket(+declared), description`. Buckets are code-true: the 8 beat-granted ids carry their declared `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` value (3 generic + 5 unlockable-generic); the rest derive `reach-gated` if `requiresReach` is set (none today — TODO(THR-515)) else `unlockable-generic` → catalog truthfully shows 161 unlockable-generic / 3 generic, including the Beats verbs imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint. Deterministic output (no timestamp; sorted category→reach→id) so the committed JSON doesn't churn (NFP #3). `public/action-catalog.html` fetches the sibling JSON and renders a grimoire-themed filter bar (search/category/reach/rarity/availability), sort toggle, and click-to-detail drawer; registered in `public/wiki-manifest.json` (moved from backlog to pages) so `generate-design-wiki` injects the shared nav. Wired into `prebuild` (mirrors `generate-ul-dashboard`/`generate-design-wiki`). Divergences from the (stale, pre-Beats) issue text: output in `public/` not `Docs/visuals/` (THR-521 moved the wiki home); `requiresSphere` isn't a real field (sphere axis carried by `sphereAffinity`); costs emitted as resolved runtime numbers. Three pillars: Engine N/A (build tooling — reads `src/` at build, adds no runtime/tick code); Content (the catalog JSON + page); UI (the served catalog surface). Verification: full suite **771 files / 11810 tests green**, `tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (generator type-clean; only pre-existing THR-489 baseline errors in `src/data/*` it imports), full `npm run build` green (prebuild chain ran the generator + dist contains both files), `check:process` green. Browser-verified at 1920×1080 (this-worktree vite:5176): 164 cards, accurate stats line, injected wiki nav (Action Catalog active), drawer opens with 17 detail rows for `action.imbue`, filter divine→19 / divine+heart→6 / reset→164; only console error a benign `favicon.ico` 404.

✅ **THR-514** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Pool-beat content templates (prose + enrichProse).** Authored the player-facing prose each cadence pool beat resolves into (THR-505 shipped only the scheduling descriptors; the modal showed generic kind copy). New `src/data/ascendant-pool-beat-templates.ts` exports `ASCENDANT_POOL_BEAT_TEMPLATES` — 14 Threadbare-voice `UnifiedActionTemplate`s (`id === beatId`, one per pool beat) with long-form `description` carrying `enrichProse` placeholders (`{name}`/`{artifact:any}`); each pool `BeatDefinition` now sets `templateId === beatId`. **Registration without leak:** the templates are deliberately kept OUT of `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` (which feeds `phaseAscendantHandFilter`/Codex/CMS/structural tests) so beats never surface as castable hand cards — instead `getUnifiedTemplateById` checks the pool as a third lookup, which is all `resolvePendingBeat`'s `templateResolver` and the modal require. **UI:** `GameView` resolves the pending pool beat's template and runs `name`+`description` through `enrichProse` against the god's anchor mortal (The First via the `courtPosition:'the_first'` thread → any threaded actor; fail-soft to kind copy when no subject so raw `{tokens}` never leak), passing a new optional `proseOverride` prop to `AscendantBeatModal` (spine beats keep `SPINE_BEAT_PRESENTATION`). **Unlock mapping:** the grant still flows from `BeatDefinition.grantsActionIds` (the THR-517 resolve contract grants from the descriptor, emitting `action.unlock.granted`, not from template aftermath) — the template's role is prose + existence (resolver) + selection-card metadata; richer template-aftermath/GraphOp resolution is TODO(THR-520). **NFP:** narrative-first authored prose (#5); additive — new file + new optional prop + a third `getUnifiedTemplateById` lookup, no existing template/contract changed (#6); fail-soft enrichment (#4). ✅ **THR-516** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Director pool draw: eligibility predicates + identity (reach/sphere) bias.** Closed the THR-505 deferral: the shipped `drawFromPool(pool, rng)` took no world state, so the Director couldn't drop a beat with nothing to say or lean toward the god's identity. Added serializable `BeatEligibility` (`always`/`unintroduced_group`/`unthreaded_target`) + `BeatIdentityBias` ({reach?, sphere?}) as optional `eligibility?`/`identity?` fields on `BeatDefinition` (additive). New exported pure evaluators in `src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts`: `isBeatEligible(beat, state)` (unintroduced_group = #culture/faction actors > #resolved introduction beats; unthreaded_target = #actor+location nodes > #ascendant `thread` edges; fail-open on throw/unknown kind) and `computeIdentityBias(beat, state)` (reach = `BEAT_REACH_BIAS_BASE + BEAT_REACH_BIAS_SLOPE × domainAffinities[reach]`; sphere = `BEAT_SPHERE_BIAS_PRIMARY/SECONDARY/NONE` on `sphereAlignment` match; reach×sphere multiplicative; 1 when no `identity`/no ascendant). `drawFromPool` gains an optional 3rd `identityBias` arg (existing two-arg callers unchanged); the Director cadence branch filters by eligibility then draws weighted by identity, and the scheduled trace's `poolSize` reflects the eligible pool. Pool beats tagged (introduction → unintroduced_group, investment → unthreaded_target). NFP: named constants (#1); 5 existing beat traces + poolSize (#2); deterministic seeded draw (#3); fail-open eligibility + getAscendantProps fail-soft (#4); additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (eligibility/bias/draw); Content (constants + pool tags; per-beat identity tags → THR-514); UI N/A (no UI file). 16 new tests (`ascendantBeatIdentity.test.ts`). Verification: full suite **11790 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (3483 baseline, THR-489). Browser-verify N/A (engine/types/data + tests only). Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-504** (2026-06-28, Onboarding & First-Run Experience) — **Scripted onboarding spine beats 0–4.** Authored the full five-beat opening arc on the Ascendant Beat substrate. **Content:** expanded `ASCENDANT_SPINE` (`src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts`) from 3 turn-gated placeholders to the complete arc with real, shipping-template grants — Beat 0 *The First* (`bind_thread_agent` + `observe_agent`), Beat 1 *The Seat* (`bind_thread_location`), Beat 2 *A Thing Left Behind* (`action.imbue`), Beat 3 *The First Word* (`divine.persuade`), Beat 4 *A Path Opens* — a `kind:'selection'` that forks the opening into one of three god-paths (`divine.dream` dreamer / `divine.omen` prophet / `divine.inspire` patron). Authored per-beat **Threadbare-voice presentation** in a new `SPINE_BEAT_PRESENTATION` map (eyebrow / title / long-form read-and-TTS prose / cta — second-person, player-as-god, indirect intervention; the scripted spine is deterministic so it carries no `enrichProse` placeholders). Extended `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` with the four `divine.*` verbs (`unlockable-generic`). New `isSpineBeatId(beatId)` helper. **UI:** `AscendantBeatModal` now prefers the authored spine copy over the generic kind-derived placeholder (falls back for pool beats); `GameView`'s auto-open + non-dismissable logic re-keyed from `kind==='spine'` to the `beat.spine.` id prefix so the closing **selection** Beat 4 also auto-opens modally and can't be dismissed (onboarding not missable). **Scope decision:** the shipped resolve path (`resolvePendingBeat`, THR-517) is deliberately **catalogue-driven** — a beat *grants action cards*; the threaded actor / throne / artifact the arc narrates are produced when the player *fires* those granted cards (The First's actor comes from the existing MeetingEncounter). The plan §4.1 richer vision — beat resolution itself running `add_node`/`setHomeSeat` aftermath to auto-seed those nodes — is an engine change to the resolve contract with judgment calls the plan doesn't pin down; deferred to THR-520 (created this session, labeled Deferral/Engine). **NFP:** all cadence/grant data are named (#1); the existing five beat traces cover the arc (#2); deterministic turn-gated spine (#3); fail-soft via the unchanged resolve path (#4); narrative-first authored prose (#5); additive — the locked THR-500/505/517 test contract (`ASCENDANT_SPINE[0]`/`[2]` by index, `beat.spine.opening` grants) is preserved unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Content (the 5-beat spine + prose + buckets); UI (modal authored-copy preference + GameView spine-prefix auto-open); Engine N/A (no engine file touched — uses the shipped Director/resolve). 6 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantSpine.test.ts`): arc shape + kinds, grants resolve to real bucketed templates, every spine beat has authored presentation, `isSpineBeatId`, Beat 4 selection (no-choice no-op / valid-choice grants exactly one), and a full Director walk 0→4 proving the player holds the opening cards by ~turn 8 with exactly one chosen path. Verification: **full suite 11774 green** (768 files, zero failures), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw. Browser-verified via this-worktree vite (5175, `?view=game&seeded&size=medium`; fresh code probed by firing the new `beat.spine.the_first_word` / `beat.spine.a_path_opens` ids that exist only in this branch): all five beats render their authored Threadbare prose modally; Beat 4 renders the choose-1-of-3 picker (Dream/Omen/Inspire with real template descriptions); selecting a path (via UI click and via `__DEBUG.resolveBeat('divine.omen')`) grants **exactly** the chosen path; OBSERVE reveals in the AscendantBar drawer after Beat 0 (unlock→drawer wiring proven); 0 console errors across the full walk. Viewport note: the host display clamps the 1920×1080 window resize to 1528×1226 via OS scaling — nothing renders below the fold; the modal fits. Unblocks the remaining Divine Cadence content (THR-514 pool prose, THR-515 card→beat wiring, THR-516 pool draw bias). PR pending.

✅ **THR-503** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Reach gate in `targetActions` + persisted ascendant reach. Both ascendant-creation paths (`createAscendant`, `createAscendantFromIdentity`) now persist `domainAffinities` on the ascendant node (`AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`); new reader `getAscendantDomainAffinities(graph, ascendantId)`. New optional `UnifiedActionTemplate.requiresReach?: ReachDomain` + tunable `REACH_GATE_MIN_AFFINITY` (0.2, `influence-content.ts`). New filter-9 reach gate in `getTargetActionSlots()`: a reach-gated card is **hidden** (like the unlock gate, never aspiration) unless the ascendant's affinity in that reach ≥ floor — a **permanent two-domain membership filter** per the director's settled premise (fixed primary+secondary reach for the whole run). Fail-soft: omitted affinities → fail-open; unknown reach → ungated + warn. Both drawer hooks (`useTargetActions`, `useAgentInteraction`) read affinities from the node. Trace gains optional `filteredByReach`. 8 new tests (6 gate + 2 persistence); full suite 11685 green, vite build green, engine smoke (seed 42 medium, tick 30, 398 agents) clean, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3483 baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080: live witness ascendant node carries `{eye:4,veil:3,shadow:2}`, served-code gate hides off-reach card / shows on-reach, 0 console errors.

New `phaseAscendantBeatDirector` orchestrator phase (registered post-doom / pre-resolution): spine-first, then cadence-gated seeded-PRNG pool draw, max-one-pending, **only offers** beats (resolution flows through the existing encounter pipeline). ✅ **THR-513** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **anoint** expression card + **chosen-faction power consumer** (completes the four-card §4.4 expression toolkit). Of the §4.4 verbs, anoint is the per-graph-type verb for **factions**, and the issue called out the genuine engine gap: THR-509 shipped `applyChosenStatusGrant` + `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` as an explicit *scaffold* ("the full mechanical payload is the consuming card's concern"), and THR-508 confirmed **nothing read** `node.properties.chosen` — so setting the chosen status alone was dead content. This issue (a) authors the verb and (b) adds the consumer that makes the chosen power mechanically live. **(a) The verb:** new `applyAnointFaction(graph, ascendantId, factionId, tick)` in `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts` validates the target is a faction node (`actor` with `actorType:'faction'`), reads the ascendant's **primary reach** (`getAscendantPrimaryReach` over persisted `domainAffinities`, THR-503 — two-domain lock), and stamps the chosen status by delegating to the shipped THR-509 `applyChosenStatusGrant` (which records the (actor × domain) power from `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE`). Dispatched from a new `'anoint_faction'` `GraphOpType` (`src/types/graphOp.ts`) intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` (sibling to `bestow_power`/`imbue_item`; deterministic — no PRNG). Fail-soft: missing faction / non-faction target / no reach each emit an `ascendant_expression` trace and no-op, never throw. **(b) The consumer:** new per-tick `phaseChosenFactionPowers` (`src/engine/chosenFactionPowers.ts`), registered as orchestrator **phase 6.56** immediately after `phaseFactionReputationDecay` (6.55) so a chosen faction's members **net upward** — it walks `getFactionNodes`, and for each non-dissolved faction carrying a `chosen.power` grants every `member_of` member a **power-keyed reputation gain** through the existing `applyFactionReputationGain` (reputation is the faction's universal lever — rank, access, bonuses, and expulsion all flow from it). v1 channels all four powers through member reputation (the issue's "member reputation/leadership bonus" example); per-power magnitude via `CHOSEN_POWER_EFFECT_TABLE` (gold *Favoured Charter* strongest at 0.005, veil/shadow subtlest at 0.002), default `CHOSEN_FACTION_REPUTATION_PER_TICK` (0.003) for powers with no table entry. **Completed `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` from 4 → all 8 reaches** (added faction powers for veil *Whispered Sanction* / eye *Illumined Charter* / stone *Enduring Foundation* / star *Fated Banner*), finishing the THR-509 partial scaffold. **Content:** authored `action.anoint` (`targetCategories:['faction']`, `essenceCost: ANOINT_COST`=6 — one tier above bestow, a standing grant lifts a whole faction every tick; Threadbare prose; unlockable-generic). New `'chosen_power'` value on the `FactionReputationTrace['cause']` union; registered `chosen_faction_power` trace category (one summary trace per chosen faction per tick) and backfilled the THR-509 `ascendant_primitive` category that anoint's chosen-status evidence depends on. **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); the per-tick consumer emits a structured summary trace + reuses the registered reputation traces (#2); arithmetic-only, no PRNG (#3); fail-soft no-op paths for resolver and consumer (#4); all additive — `applyChosenStatusGrant`, `applyFactionReputationGain`, and the existing intercepts are unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyAnointFaction` + the `phaseChosenFactionPowers` consumer); Content (the `action.anoint` template + completed power table); UI (renders through the existing generic AscendantHand/ActionDrawer once unlocked — no new component; card→beat-grant wiring is THR-515). 16 new tests: 8 resolver (`ascendantExpression.test.ts` — power selection by primary reach, all-8-reaches map, re-anoint latest-patron-wins, three fail-soft) + 8 consumer (`chosenFactionPowers.test.ts` — gain applied, per-power magnitude, multi-tick accumulation, clamp at 1.0, default fallback, un-anointed no-op, dissolved skip, no-members no-throw); repointed 1 stale THR-509 test (it asserted `star` → null power; now that all reaches map, the null-power fail-soft path is exercised via a non-actor node type). Verification: full suite **11768 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw; `action.anoint` passes the `unified-action-templates.test.ts` structural validation in the green run. Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components`/`src/views`/HexMapV2 surface touched — the card surfaces through the existing generic drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants. Systemic-wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-512** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **bestow power** expression card + agent-boon subsystem. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs (imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint), bestow is the per-graph-type verb for **agents**, and the largest of the four because the plan flagged it as needing a genuinely-new consumer: "no agent-castable-ability store exists." The issue posed two open design questions, both resolved here. **(1) Agent-ability store — option (a) over option (b):** rather than build a new agent-side ability store + casting hook (option b, bigger), bestow **mints a "Divine Gift" `artifact` node and binds it to the agent with a `possesses` edge** (option a — "cheapest, keeps it wired"), reusing the imbue/effect-walker path end-to-end. The gift carries two `AttachmentEffect`s read by walkers that already ship, so the boon genuinely applies (not dead content) with zero new consumer subsystem: a `passive` reach bonus in the ascendant's **primary reach** (new `getAscendantPrimaryReach` reads the highest-affinity entry of `AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`, persisted by THR-503) summed by `collectAttachmentEffects`/`effectResolver` into the holder's reach modifiers; and a per-tick `resource_manipulate` quintessence restore applied each tick by `tickEffects`/`tickResourceManipulate` (`effectTick.ts`). **(2) Quintessence-regen semantic** (flagged "resolve in the issue" in plan §4.4): quintessence is a 0–1 health-scale value, not an accumulator, so the boon is a **regen-rate boost** via the per-tick `resource_manipulate` effect, **clamped to the holder's `quintessenceMax`** by `tickResourceManipulate` — never overfills. **Awareness gate:** thread `awareness` lives on the **thread edge** (`'unaware'|'intuition'|'faith'|'communion'`), not a node property, so it can't be a `requiredNodeProperties` filter; gated fail-soft **at resolution** — read the `thread` edge (`getOutgoingEdges(ascendantId,'thread').find(e=>e.target===agentId)`) and no-op when `awareness < BESTOW_MIN_AWARENESS` (`'faith'`). **Engine:** new `applyBestowPower(graph, ascendantId, agentId, tick)` + `getAscendantPrimaryReach` in `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts`; new `'bestow_power'` `GraphOpType` (`src/types/graphOp.ts`) intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` (sibling to `imbue_item`; deterministic — no PRNG, the effects are fixed). Fail-soft cases (missing agent / non-actor / no thread / insufficient awareness / no reach) each emit an `ascendant_expression` trace and no-op, never throw. **Content:** authored `action.bestow` (`targetCategories:['agent']`, `essenceCost: BESTOW_COST`=5, Threadbare prose, unlockable-generic). New constants `BESTOW_COST`/`BESTOW_REACH_BONUS`/`BESTOW_QUINTESSENCE_REGEN`/`BESTOW_MIN_AWARENESS` (`ascendant-expression-constants.ts`). **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); structured trace incl. fail-soft markers, reuses the registered `ascendant_expression` category (#2); deterministic, no PRNG (#3); five fail-soft no-op paths (#4); all additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyBestowPower`); Content (the `action.bestow` template); UI (renders through the existing generic AscendantHand/ActionDrawer once unlocked — no new component; card→beat-grant wiring is THR-515). 15 new tests (`ascendantExpression.test.ts`): `getAscendantPrimaryReach` highest-affinity + fail-soft; gift-artifact + `possesses` creation with both effects; communion passes / intuition + unset fail the faith gate; four fail-soft cases; and **two integration proofs** — `collectAttachmentEffects` surfaces the reach passive for the holder, and `tickEffects` actually restores quintessence by `BESTOW_QUINTESSENCE_REGEN` (and clamps at `quintessenceMax`). Verification: full suite **11753 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components`/`src/views`/HexMapV2 surface touched — the card surfaces through the existing generic drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants. Systemic-wiring guide updated (the bestow agent-targeting "mint a possessed gift" pattern, distinct from the imbue GraphOp-intercept + consecrate sustained-control patterns). PR pending.

✅ **THR-511** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **consecrate** sustained-control spawn bridge + card. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs, consecrate is the per-graph-type verb for **locations**: it converts a temple/shrine into a faith-spreading site via a sustained `ControlEffect`. The blocker (called out in plan §4.4 and the THR-508 split note): every prior sustained control was **hex-targeted**, but consecrate targets a location node, and `spawnControlEffect` neither accepted a location target nor carried the THR-509 spec fields. The consumer side already shipped and was tested in THR-509 — `phaseControlEffects` reads `effect.perTickThreadAuras` + `effect.targetNodeId` and applies `applyCoLocatedThreadAura`, and `getUpkeepStatus` handles `upkeepArtifactId` — so the only gap was the **establishment bridge**. **Engine:** extended `spawnControlEffect` (`src/engine/controlEffectSpawn.ts`) with an optional `graph` param and a location-target branch — when the target id is not a `hex_COL_ROW` but resolves to a `location` node with numeric `hexCol`/`hexRow`, it anchors the effect on those coords and sets `targetNodeId = locationId`; both target shapes now carry `spec.perTickThreadAuras` + `spec.upkeepArtifactId` onto the spawned effect. Fail-soft: a non-hex target with no graph, a location missing hex coords, or a non-location node all return null (no effect, no throw). The 3 call sites in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` pass `state.graph`. **Content:** authored `action.consecrate` (`durationMode:'sustained'` + `controlSpec` with `perTickCost {spirit: CONSECRATE_PERTICK}` and `perTickThreadAuras [{magnitude: CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK}]` faith-spread, `targetCategories:['location']`, `targetSubtypes:['temple','shrine']`, upfront `essenceCost: CONSECRATE_ESTABLISH_COST`). New constants `CONSECRATE_ESTABLISH_COST`/`CONSECRATE_PERTICK` in `ascendant-expression-constants.ts`; reuses the existing `CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`. **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); the spawn trace now carries `targetNodeId` (#2); arithmetic-only resolution (#3); the four fail-soft null paths above (#4); purely additive — the existing hex-target path and all 13 prior `controlEffectSpawn` tests are unchanged, only the optional param and a new branch were added (#6). Three pillars: Engine (the spawn bridge); Content (the consecrate template); UI N/A (the card→drawer/bucket wiring is THR-515; no new UI surface). 5 new tests in `controlEffectSpawn.test.ts`: location-target resolves coords + sets `targetNodeId` + carries auras; fail-soft no-graph / no-coords / wrong-node-type; and an end-to-end test feeding the spawned effect through `phaseControlEffects` that proves a co-located threaded pilgrim's `ticksAtCurrentTier` advances by the aura magnitude. Plus a passthrough test asserting both THR-509 fields ride through. Verification: full suite **11740 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw. Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components` or HexMapV2 surface touched (the card surfaces in the drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants + `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS`). **Deferral:** the **relic-mint variant** (high upfront `CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT` mints a permanent `lossCondition:'permanent'` artifact and injects its *dynamic* id into the effect's `upkeepArtifactId` for zero ongoing upkeep) needs a new mint + dynamic-id-injection path the static `controlSpec.upkeepArtifactId` can't express — split to THR-518. The bridge already carries the field through, so THR-518 is purely the minting card. Systemic-wiring guide updated (the consecrate sustained-control authoring pattern, distinct from the imbue GraphOp-intercept pattern). PR pending.

✅ **THR-517** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #8) — **Beat resolve path: offer→enter→resolve wiring.** The Director (THR-500) only *offered* beats — it set `ascendantBeats.pending` but nothing in the running sim ever cleared it, so no beat (spine or pool) was playable. This closes the loop. **Engine:** new `resolvePendingBeat(state, {chosenActionId?, outcome?}, templateResolver?)` in `src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts` — looks the pending beat up across spine ∪ pool ∪ delivery (new exported `getBeatDefinitionById`, wrapping the private `findBeatDefinition`), applies its grants into `state.unlockedActionIds` (dedup; one `action.unlock.granted` trace `via: 'beat'` per newly-revealed id), then records the `BeatRecord` + clears `pending` by delegating to the existing, already-tested pure `resolveAscendantBeat` (left untouched). Selection beats (`kind:'selection'`) grant exactly one option — `chosenActionId` must be one of the beat's `grantsActionIds`, else it's a no-op (`resolved:false`, the beat stays pending so the player must choose); non-selection beats grant all of `grantsActionIds`. **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** nothing pending → no-op; a pending beat whose definition is unknown (stale save / removed catalogue entry) or whose declared `templateId` fails the injected `templateResolver` clears gracefully via a new `ascendant.beat.skipped` reason `'missing_template'` (the `BeatSkippedTrace` union extended additively) so the queue never wedges; the whole body is try/caught (emits `engine_warning`, leaves the beat pending) so a thrown resolver can't crash a tick. The engine stays registry-free — GameView injects `(id) => getUnifiedTemplateById(id) !== undefined` at the UI boundary. **UI** (new `src/components/Game/AscendantBeatModal.tsx`, mirrors `StoryBeatModal`): a pending pool/optional beat surfaces as a top-center thread-tug **offer banner** (`AscendantBeatOfferBanner`, z-40, simplified THR-340 handoff) the player clicks to enter; spine beats **auto-open modally** (onboarding not missable, gated by `interruptsSuppressed`, non-dismissable). The entered-beat modal renders a kind-derived eyebrow/title/prose placeholder (real Threadbare prose is content — TODO(THR-514)) plus either a single **Receive** CTA (non-selection, shows "You will learn: …" with grant names resolved via `getUnifiedTemplateById`) or a generic **choose-1-of-N picker** (selection) — each option a card with the template's name + description; resolving flows through `handleResolveBeat` → `resolvePendingBeat` → `setGameState`, and the granted card simply appears in the drawer (which already filters on `unlockedActionIds`). **Debug:** `__DEBUG.resolveBeat(chosenActionId?)` (new `DebugResolveBeatResult`; added to `BeatBridge` in `debug-bridge.ts` + the `DebugBridge`/`_registerBeatBridge` shapes in `debug-bridge.d.ts`) resolves the pending beat headlessly via the same engine path; a BeatsDebugTab "Enter & Resolve" control (with a chosen-action-id input for selection beats) drives it from the panel. **NFP:** outcome strings/`missing_template` are inspectable (#2); pure over inputs aside from trace emission, no PRNG (#3); fail-soft table above (#4); all additive — the pure `resolveAscendantBeat` and existing tests are unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Engine (`resolvePendingBeat` + `getBeatDefinitionById`); Content N/A here (grant-only beats; prose/encounter-template handoff is THR-514); UI (modal + banner + picker + debug control). 7 new engine tests (`ascendantBeat.test.ts`): grant-all + history + traces, selection needs-a-valid-choice / grants-exactly-one / rejects-bad-choice, no-duplicate-grant, no-op-when-nothing-pending, fail-soft unknown-beat → `missing_template`, fail-soft templateId-resolver-false → `missing_template`. Verification: full suite **11734 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5174, `?view=game&seeded&size=medium`, fresh-code probed via `__DEBUG.resolveBeat` existence): fired `beat.spine.opening` → modal auto-opened ('A DIVINE CADENCE / Opening' → Receive), `resolveBeat()` → `pending` cleared, `unlockedActionIds` grew `[]→[bind_thread_agent, observe_agent]`, history recorded the `BeatRecord` (outcome 'received'); fired `beat.pool.select.first_true_gift` → offer banner → entered → 3-option picker → clicking 'Imbue' granted **only** `action.imbue` (outcome 'chosen'), unlock set 2→3; **0 console errors** (38 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). Plumbing only — the prose-rich encounter-screen path for template-backed beats is content (TODO(THR-514)). Unblocks THR-504 (scripted spine beats) + THR-514 (pool prose + delivery encounter handoff). Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-506** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Delivery-beat adapter for THR-452 branching content. New `src/engine/deliveryBeatAdapter.ts` maps each registered branching encounter (`LOCATION_BRANCHING_ENCOUNTER_TEMPLATES` — 23 of THR-452's ~30 templates that never fire in normal sim because their reputation/mark/court prereqs are never matured by ambient mortal pathing) into a `delivery` `BeatDefinition`: id `beat.delivery.<sourceId>`, `templateId` = the source encounter, `cadence` trigger, and a normalised `DELIVERY_BEAT_WEIGHT` (0.1) so the 23-entry group stays ~10% of pool draws rather than swamping the base pool (a per-beat weight of 1 would give delivery ~half of all draws). The point of a delivery beat is to **present the encounter to the player-god as a divine vision** — the Director hosts it directly, *sidestepping the mortal-pathing prereqs* that make it unreachable — so `eligibleDeliveryBeats(history)` filters only on dedup-against-already-delivered (the live "currently-valid" gate), deliberately **not** re-applying the encounter's own gates. The Director (`ascendantBeat.ts`) merges those eligible delivery beats into the cadence draw pool (the base `ASCENDANT_BEAT_POOL` stays delivery-free, so the THR-505 pool tests are untouched); `forceOfferBeatById` extended to host delivery beats; `BeatScheduledTrace`/`BeatOfferedTrace` gain optional `templateId` so traces name the wrapped encounter. A new CLI `beat` command (`status` / `list [delivery]` / `fire <beatId>`) is the headless equivalent of THR-507's browser `__DEBUG.fireBeat`; `__DEBUG.listBeats` adds a `delivery` source and `beatSchedule.eligiblePool` reflects the merged set. Resolution (offer→enter→resolve) is still THR-514, so delivery beats fire via force-offer today and naturally once that lands. **Done-when demonstrated:** in a seed-42 CLI run, `beat fire beat.delivery.enc.courtyard_duel` offered the delivery beat with `source template: enc.courtyard_duel` and emitted `ascendant.beat.scheduled … → enc.courtyard_duel`. 9 new tests (`deliveryBeatAdapter.test.ts`): adapter mapping (kind/trigger/templateId/weight/unique ids, no spine/pool collision), id round-trip, eligibility dedup, merged-pool reachability, and force-offer trace carrying the source `templateId`. Verification: full suite **11727 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (THR-489 red baseline — all flagged lines pre-existing, none in the changed code). Browser-verify exempt: engine/data/CLI + types only (the debug-bridge change is a headless read, no UI render). PR pending.

✅ **THR-505** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Pool beat starter library + unlock catalog. Populated the empty `ASCENDANT_BEAT_POOL` (`src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts`) with 14 cadence-triggered beats (6 introduction / 6 investment / 2 selection) so the Director surfaces living-world beats once the spine is exhausted instead of skipping `empty_pool`; added the §4.3 unlock catalogue — `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` (generic / unlockable-generic / reach-gated + `requiresReach` field for future cards) plus `collectGrantedActionIds()` deriving `beatId→grants` from the `BeatDefinition`s (no drift-prone second map). All grants grounded in **real shipping templates only** (`bind_thread_agent`/`bind_thread_location`/`observe_agent`/`action.imbue`) — no vapor ids. Authored against the **shipped** minimal `BeatDefinition`/`drawFromPool` shape (no eligibility-predicate or identity-bias fields exist on the descriptor; `drawFromPool` takes no world state) → deferred: prose-rich templates + offer→enter→resolve UI path (THR-514), pool/bucket expansion as consecrate/bestow/anoint + reach-gated cards ship (THR-515), eligibility + identity (reach/sphere) draw bias needing a Director draw-signature change (THR-516). 9 new tests (`ascendantBeatPool.test.ts`): Director draws ≥3 distinct pool beats over a deterministic 60-tick run, catalogue covers every granted action, every granted + bucketed id resolves to a real `UnifiedActionTemplate`. Verification: full suite **11718 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3483 baseline). Browser-verify exempt: content/data-only change (`src/data` + tests), no UI surface touched. PR pending.

✅ **THR-508** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **imbue** expression card. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs (imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint), imbue is the one that composes the already-shipped THR-509 primitives end-to-end with **zero new consumer wiring** — so it ships fully-wired here and the other three split into THR-511/512/513 (the split plan §4.4 explicitly anticipated). **Engine/content:** new `action.imbue` `UnifiedActionTemplate` (`targetCategories:['artifact','artifact_legendary']`, `essenceCost: IMBUE_ESSENCE_COST`=4, an unlockable-generic card hidden until a beat grants it) in `ATTACHMENT_ACTION_TEMPLATES`; its `onSuccess` fires a new `imbue_item` GraphOp type, intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts`'s `executeStepResult` (sibling to `faction_verb`/`anoint_successor`/`plant_schism`) and dispatched to `applyImbueItem` (new `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts`). `applyImbueItem` reads the ascendant's **primary sphere** (`getAscendantPrimarySphere`, off `AscendantProperties.sphereAlignment.primary`), picks a sphere-flavored `AttachmentEffect` via THR-509 `pickSphereFlavoredEffect` fed a locally-derived seeded PRNG (`mulberry32(seed + tick·53 + hash(actor) + hash(target))` — deterministic, doesn't disturb the resolution rng stream), and **appends it to the target artifact node's `properties.effects`**. **Wiring proof (not dead content):** `collectAttachmentEffects` walks an agent's `possesses` edges and reads `properties.effects` off the held artifact, so an imbued artifact's new power applies to whoever carries it — an integration test asserts `collectAttachmentEffects` surfaces the imbued effect for the possessing mortal. New `ascendant_expression` trace category registered in both `TraceCategory` union + `TRACE_CATEGORIES` (THR-509's `ascendant_primitive` was emitted via `as never` and never registered — fixed for this one). **NFP:** `IMBUE_ESSENCE_COST` named constant (#1, new `src/data/ascendant-expression-constants.ts`); structured trace incl. fail-soft markers (#2); injected seeded PRNG (#3); fail-soft no-op + trace on missing/non-artifact target or sphereless ascendant, never throws (#4); all additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyImbueItem`); Content (the `action.imbue` template + sphere→effect flavoring); UI (renders through the existing ActionCard/drawer once unlocked — no new component; data+engine change, UI-exempt by the file-path rule, still browser-verified). 10 new tests (`ascendantExpression.test.ts`): sphere-match, append-not-replace, determinism, three fail-soft cases, and the effect-walker integration. Verification: full suite **11709 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5191): a dynamic `import('/src/data/unified-action-templates.ts')` confirmed `action.imbue` live in *this* build with `onSuccess` op `imbue_item` and correct fields (freshness-probe per the worktree-preview caveat); `__DEBUG.grantUnlock('action.imbue')` accepted; the drawer correctly shows only the turn-1 Core floor (Move+Investiture) with imbue hidden until unlocked; 0 console errors (66 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). **Split:** `consecrate` → THR-511 (needs a location-targeted sustained-control spawn bridge — `spawnControlEffect` is hex-only and doesn't carry the THR-509 `perTickThreadAuras`/`upkeepArtifactId` fields), `bestow` → THR-512 (needs an agent-castable-ability subsystem — no agent ability store exists; + the unresolved quintessence-regen semantic), `anoint` → THR-513 (needs a `chosen`-status power consumer — confirmed nothing reads `properties.chosen`; THR-509's `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` is an explicit scaffold). Systemic-wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-463** (2026-06-22, Encounter Experience) — interaction-gated camera centering. `cameraInteractionGate.ts` pure predicate; camera pans to the followed agent only on a pending encounter/aftermath or a populated arrival. `'camera_center'` TraceCategory + `CameraCenterTrace`. PR #368.

✅ **THR-462** (2026-06-13, Encounter Experience) — propagate `narrativeTag` into `EncounterNotification` for the near-miss amber toast; `EncounterAftermathSummary.narrativeTag` stored from resolution, passed via `buildEncounterNotification`. PR #341.

✅ **THR-501** (2026-06-27, Action System & Unlocks) — Turn-1 floor = Move + Investiture across every action surface; retired the legacy intervention wheel + gated the agent hand and AscendantBar Self/Rare so the floor holds coherently (not a half-state). Substrate: `STARTER_ACTION_IDS=[]`/`STARTER_ACTION_COUNT=0`, stripped `starter:true` from the 12 ex-Starter-12 templates (empty floor + `isActionRevealed` hide every former starter until a beat unlocks it). Agent hand: retired `getAgentWheelSlots` (tier-based radial wheel, no unlock concept, on the rejected-approaches list; flagged `@deprecated`); interventions now flow through unified `divine.*` templates via `getTargetActionSlots` (curated `AGENT_INTERVENTION_TEMPLATES` = 8 divine + observe + thread-creation), now passing `unlockedActionIds` (was omitted → the gate's backward-compat branch revealed all). AscendantBar: `selectActionTray` gates Self/Rare behind the unlock set + `ActionsBlock` hides empty tier groups (Core / Move+Investiture hardcoded, always-on). 11699 tests green, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 398 agents) clean, `tsc --noEmit` clean; browser-verified at 1920×1080 (`__DEBUG.listStarterActions()===[]`, AscendantBar Core·2 only, Kael selected → no intervention cards, 0 console errors). Blocks THR-504 (spine beats deliver the unlocks). PR pending.

✅ **THR-507** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #8) — `__DEBUG` beat controls + DebugPanel Beats tab. The inspection/test surface for the Director (THR-500): four headless `window.__DEBUG` functions and a live debug tab, so beats can be driven and observed without waiting for natural cadence. **Engine:** new exported `forceOfferBeatById(beats, beatId, turn)` in `src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts` — reuses the Director's internal `offer()` (same scheduled/offered traces, same cursor logic) to force-offer any spine/pool beat by id, advancing the spine cursor only when the fired beat is the one the cursor points at (so debug-firing a passed/future spine beat never corrupts the cursor); returns `null` for unknown ids. **Debug bridge (`src/debug-bridge.{ts,d.ts}`):** `listBeats()` (catalogue: spine + pool with kind/trigger/grants/weight), `beatSchedule()` (live Director snapshot: turn, spineCursor/length, nextSpineBeatId, lastBeatTurn, pending, eligible pool, run-unlocked set, history) — both read-only via `_gameStateProvider` + a dynamic content import; `fireBeat(beatId)` and `grantUnlock(actionId)` are mutating, delegated to a new `_beatBridge` registered by GameView. `grantUnlock` pushes into the existing `unlockedActionIds` (the same set `unlock_action` writes) and emits `action.unlock.granted` with `via: 'debug'` (the trace type already allowed it). **UI:** new `BeatsDebugTab.tsx` + a `Beats` tab in `DebugTabContent` — self-contained (reads via the four `__DEBUG` fns, no prop plumbing; mirrors `ActionUnlocksView`), re-reads on tick change, renders Director state / pending beat / fire+grant controls / run-unlocked list / spine catalogue (with ▶ cursor marker) / pool / history. **NFP:** the only randomness path (`offer` jitter) is untouched/seeded (#3); `forceOfferBeatById` returns `null` rather than throwing on unknown ids and the bridge fns no-op when the game isn't loaded (#4); all changes additive (#6); inspectability is the entire point (#2). Three pillars: Engine (`forceOfferBeatById`); Content N/A (no prose — this is dev tooling); UI (the Beats tab). 3 new tests (force-offer + trace emission, cursor-advance-only-at-cursor, unknown-id → null); existing 14-test beat suite green. Verification: full suite **11699 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, `tsc -b` exits 0 with only the THR-489 baseline errors (none in touched files). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5174): all four `__DEBUG` fns served + working headlessly (`fireBeat('beat.spine.opening')` → pending set + spineCursor 0→1; `grantUnlock('invest.thread_actor')` → run-unlock set grows), Beats tab renders the live state, **0 console errors** (38 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). Wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-502** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #3) — Home seat (throne) + mana income. The throne is the ascendant's seat of power: a location flagged on the god that yields steady essence and shows a signifier on the map. This is the engine + UI half; the *setting* of the seat in normal play is the scripted spine beat's job (child #5). **Engine/state:** new optional `AscendantProperties.homeSeatLocationId` (`src/types/influence.ts`) — additive, a run without a seat omits it. New tunable `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT` (1.0) in `src/data/influence-content.ts`, re-exported from `influence.ts` and exposed in both CMS surfaces (`registry.ts` + `tunableConstants.ts`, NFP #1). Both essence computations gain one seat term: the engine generator `computeEssenceGeneration` (influence.ts) and the view-layer HUD mirror `computeEssenceIncome` (essenceIncome.ts) each add `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT` for the location named by `homeSeatLocationId` **only when it resolves to a live node** (fail-soft §3.8: a seat pointing at a destroyed location contributes 0), and **exclude that same location from the place-of-power loop** so the seat is a *named higher-yield place of power* that replaces (not stacks with) the 0.5 place-of-power bonus — threading a fresh non-place-of-power location as the seat raises per-tick essence by exactly `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT`. **Throne modeling:** a `location` node + a `controls` edge (ascendant → location), **not a new node type** (load-bearing rule honored). New `setHomeSeat(graph, ascendantId, locationRef?)` engine helper sets the property + ensures the controls edge (deterministic edge id, guarded against duplicates); `locationRef` resolves by exact id → id prefix → exact name → name-substring, and when omitted auto-picks capital → city → first location. **Decision (noted for spine beat #5):** the plan also names a `thread` edge from the ascendant to the seat location; this was deliberately **not** created — thread edges are actor-targeting (carry `ThreadEdgeProperties` tier/maintenance and are iterated by maintenance/promotion/thread-digest/UI as if the target is a mortal), so rippling them onto location nodes is left to #5 with full cross-system analysis. This also keeps the income delta exactly `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT`. **Debug (NFP #2):** `setHomeSeat` wired into the encounter debug bridge (`GameView` — has `touchStructure`/`setGameState` in scope) and exposed as `__DEBUG.setHomeSeat(locationRef?)` (+ `.d.ts` type). **UI:** HexMapV2 seat signifier — a gold ring + crown texture (`buildSeatMarkerTexture`/`addSeatMarker` in `LocationIconMesh.ts`, mirroring the capital-ring pattern, rendered above the base icon and capital ring) keyed off the new `LocationNode.isHomeSeat`, which GameView's `locationNodes` memo sets by comparing each node id to the ascendant's `homeSeatLocationId` (memo now also depends on `gameState.ascendantId`; refreshes via `structuralCacheVersion` which `touchStructure` bumps). **NFP:** seat yield is a named constant (#1); deterministic (#3); fail-soft on missing seat/location (#4); all changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (field + income term on both paths + `setHomeSeat`); Content N/A (no prose — the seat is set mechanically; the constant is the authoring knob); UI (HexMapV2 signifier + auto-reflecting essence HUD via the income mirror). 10 new tests — seat income on both paths (exact-delta, no-stack-with-place-of-power, fail-soft-missing-location), `setHomeSeat` (property + controls edge + raised generation, capital-preferred default, fail-soft no-locations), and `LocationIconMesh` (+1 seat sprite for `isHomeSeat`, crown-path no-throw). Verification: full suite **11696 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (the one `essenceIncome` unused-local at GameView:1275 is the pre-existing memo, untouched; THR-489 baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5173): `__DEBUG.setHomeSeat()` → "Ardenmor Keep" (loc_0), "+1 essence/tick"; the seat hex renders the gold ring + crown signifier (confirmed on Thornhaven when re-seated there); 0 console errors. Observation: changing the seat rebuilds the location layer (existing locations-array-change behavior) → a one-time camera-zoom reset — acceptable since the spine sets the seat once early in normal play. Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-503** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #4) — Reach gate in `targetActions` + persisted ascendant reach. The ascendant's reach affinities lived only in the Remembrance `AscendantIdentity` and `targetActions.ts` had only a sphere gate; this persists the reaches on the graph node and adds the matching reach gate so reach-locked investment cards (catalog #6, future) can be authored. **Engine/state:** both creation paths (`createAscendant` from `archetype.startingDomainAffinities`, `createAscendantFromIdentity` from `identity.domainAffinities`) now write `domainAffinities` onto the ascendant node via the new optional `AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`; new reader `getAscendantDomainAffinities(graph, ascendantId)` makes the node the single source of truth post-creation. New optional `UnifiedActionTemplate.requiresReach?: ReachDomain` (additive, backward compatible) and tunable `REACH_GATE_MIN_AFFINITY` (0.2) in `src/data/influence-content.ts`. New **filter-9 reach gate** in `getTargetActionSlots()`, placed right after the unlock gate: when a template declares `requiresReach` and affinities are supplied, the card is **hidden entirely** (a `continue`, like the unlock gate — never shown dimmed as aspiration) unless the ascendant's affinity in that reach ≥ the floor. This realizes the director's settled **two-domain lock** (2026-06-26 refinement comment): every ascendant holds a fixed primary+secondary reach for the whole run, so the threshold over the fixed-at-creation affinity map is mathematically a permanent set-membership check (present reaches score 2–5 ≫ 0.2; absent reaches resolve to 0). **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** affinities omitted → gate skipped (fail-open — tests / no-ascendant contexts see all reach cards); `requiresReach` not in `REACH_DOMAINS` → ungated + `console.warn`. **Inspectability (NFP #2):** `FilterCounts.byReach` + optional `TargetActionFilterTrace.filteredByReach`; the filter trace now also emits when reach-hides occur. Both drawer hooks (`useTargetActions`, `useAgentInteraction`) read affinities from the node and pass `ascendantDomainAffinities`. **NFP:** floor is a named constant (#1); deterministic pure filter (#3); all type changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (gate + persistence + reader); Content (`requiresReach` is the new authoring field — added to the systemic-wiring-guide so authors know reach-gating exists); UI (drawer hooks thread the value; browser-verified the drawer still renders + the live witness ascendant node carries `{eye:4,veil:3,shadow:2}`). 8 new tests (6 gate: hidden-off-reach / visible-on-reach / below-floor-hidden / fail-open / ungated-always-visible, + 2 persistence/reader). Verification: full suite **11685 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 baseline == 3483 with changes; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5179): 0 console errors (66 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings), served-code gate hides off-reach card / shows on-reach + ungated, live ascendant node carries persisted affinities. Systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-509** (2026-06-27) — Reusable ascendant action primitives (Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence, child issue; blocks the THR-508 expression cards). Built the four general-purpose building blocks the imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint cards (and future ascendant verbs) sit on, *before* the cards, so authoring stays cheap — the verb is universal, the magic it produces is flavored by the ascendant's domain + sphere. All four live in new `src/engine/ascendantPrimitives.ts`, are pure (graph mutation aside), fail-soft (unknown node/sphere/artifact → no-op + warn, never throw), deterministic (only the sphere picker randomizes, via an injected seeded PRNG), and emit an `ascendant_primitive` trace. **(1) `relic_upkeep_substitute`** — `getUpkeepStatus(effect, graph)` classifies a control effect's upkeep against its sustaining relic (`no_relic`/`waived`/`relic_lost`); `ControlSpec` + `ControlEffect` gain an additive `upkeepArtifactId?`, and `phaseControlEffects` now waives `perTickCost` each tick while that artifact exists and lapses the effect with a new `LapseReason` `'upkeep_relic_destroyed'` when it's gone — the engine half of "mint a permanent relic that sustains a consecration with zero ongoing upkeep." **(2) `co_located_thread_aura`** — `applyCoLocatedThreadAura(graph, locationId, ascendantId, spec, tick)`, a location-scoped per-tick mutation that, for every threaded agent co-located with the target location (and, by default, its `contains`-child sublocations — the three-tier hex→location→sublocation model), adds `spec.magnitude` to a thread-edge field (default `ticksAtCurrentTier`, which drives `checkTierPromotion`). It rides new `ControlEffect.perTickThreadAuras` and is applied every tick by `phaseControlEffects`; first consumer is consecrate's faith-spread (`CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`). **(3) `chosen_status_grant`** — `applyChosenStatusGrant(graph, nodeId, domain, byAscendantId, tick)` flags a node `chosen` (writes `node.properties.chosen` with patron/domain/tick/power) and records a power picked from a `(NodeType × ReachDomain)` `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` scaffold (factions are `actor` nodes; iron→leadership / heart→faith / gold→reputation / shadow→veiled, others null-power but still flagged); re-granting overwrites (latest patron wins) and reports `alreadyChosen`. First consumer: anoint. **(4) `sphere_flavored_effect`** — `pickSphereFlavoredEffect(sphere, rng, tick?)` reads `SPHERE_EFFECT_TABLE` (8 of 12 spheres scaffolded → thematic passive reach bonuses) and picks one `AttachmentEffect` via the injected `() => number` seeded PRNG (`mulberry32(seed)`), so one verb (imbue) yields domain-appropriate magic. The shared spec type (`CoLocatedThreadAuraSpec`) + tuning constants (`CO_LOCATED_AURA_DEFAULT_FIELD`, `CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`) live in new `src/types/ascendantPrimitives.ts` to avoid an engine→types import cycle from `controlEffect.ts`, and are re-exported from the engine module so consumers (cards, tests, the control phase) have a single import site. **Placement decision (noted for THR-508):** the issue's "new effect kind in the effect union" was realized as reusable resolvers + a per-tick `ControlEffect` rider rather than new members of the 40-type `AttachmentEffect` union or the aftermath-reaction union — this keeps the change fully additive (no ripple through the giant effect resolver, no mutex collision with THR-508's card work) and makes each primitive unit-testable in isolation; THR-508 wires the cards to these resolvers (and may add thin aftermath wrappers then). **NFP:** every magnitude/value is a named constant (#1); each resolver traces, incl. fail-soft markers (#2); the only randomness is an injected seeded PRNG (#3); missing targets no-op + warn (#4); all type changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (the four primitives + phase wiring); Content (the `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` / `SPHERE_EFFECT_TABLE` scaffolds + systemic-wiring-guide entry are the authoring surface); UI N/A this foundation (no player-facing surface — browser-verify exempt: engine plumbing, no UI render). 18 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantPrimitives.test.ts`) cover each primitive in isolation plus a faith-spread→tier-promotion integration (a tier-2 thread promotes to tier-3 via accumulated devotion over ticks — the issue's "Done when" CLI-smoke proof) and relic waive/lapse through `phaseControlEffects`. Verification: full suite **11678 green**, `vite build` green, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (the only two errors in touched files — a `checkThreshold` cast and an unused test import — are pre-existing on `origin/main`, THR-489 baseline), and the 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42, medium) reached tick 30 with 398 agents and no throw. Systemic wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-500** (2026-06-26) — Ascendant Beat Director foundation (Divine Cadence substrate; child issue #1 of the Ascendant Beats project, blocks the rest). An *Ascendant Beat* is an encounter addressed to the player-god rather than resolved between mortals ("The First" is the hand-wired archetype). This lays the engine substrate: a Director that decides *which* beat fires *when*, the flat state it keeps, and the aftermath effect that turns a resolved beat into a new player capability. **Engine:** new `phaseAscendantBeatDirector` orchestrator phase (`src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts`), registered inline after the post-doom slot and before encounter resolution — a pure `(state, rng)` function that (1) no-ops while a beat is pending (max-one-pending), (2) offers the next scripted spine beat once its trigger is met (advancing `spineCursor`, with `-1` marking spine exhaustion), else (3) does a cadence-gated, seeded-PRNG weighted pool draw. It **only offers** (sets `pending` + emits traces); resolution flows through the existing encounter pipeline, and the exported `resolveAscendantBeat` closes the loop (clears `pending`, appends a `BeatRecord`, emits `ascendant.beat.resolved`). New flat `GameState.ascendantBeats?: AscendantBeatState` (spineCursor/pending/history/lastBeatTurn) — additive/optional so old saves + fixtures fail soft (the Director no-ops when absent); types in new `src/types/ascendantBeat.ts` (`AscendantBeatState`, `PendingBeat`, `BeatRecord`, `BeatKind`, `BeatTrigger`, `BeatDefinition`); init via `createInitialAscendantBeatState()` in `gameInit`. New `unlock_action` aftermath effect (`EncounterAftermathReactionEffect` union member in `unifiedAction.ts`; resolved in `encounterAftermath.ts`): pushes its `actionId` into the run-scoped unlock set, dedup/idempotent, fail-soft on an unknown id (harmless — no template matches so nothing reveals), emits `action.unlock.granted`. **Key coordination decision:** the plan named a new `runUnlockedActionIds` field, but the handoff overrode with "additive fields only on `gameState.ts`" + "mutex with THR-501 (unlock field)"; since the existing `unlockedActionIds` **is** the run-scoped set the action drawer already filters via `isActionRevealed()`, `unlock_action` writes to it directly rather than renaming a 176-importer field — the rename + 2-generic floor init stays THR-501's scope. **Content:** tunables + catalogue in new `src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts` (`BEAT_BASE_INTERVAL` 9, `BEAT_INTERVAL_JITTER` ±2, `BEAT_MIN_GAP` 4, `BEAT_MAX_PENDING` 1, `SPINE_TRIGGER_TURNS` [0,2,4,6,8], `BEAT_KIND_WEIGHTS`) + a minimal turn-gated spine and an empty pool — the rich prose-first spine (Beats 0–4) and starter pool library are deferred to the child content issues. **Inspectability (NFP #2):** 5 trace categories (`ascendant.beat.{scheduled,offered,skipped,resolved}`, `action.unlock.granted`) added to `TraceCategory`, `TRACE_CATEGORIES`, and the `TraceEntry` union with typed interfaces; a small `emitBeatTrace` wrapper localizes the one cast needed because `Omit<TraceEntry, 'id'|'timestamp'>` collapses the union to base keys. **NFP:** all cadence/spine numbers named constants (#1); seeded PRNG `mulberry32(seed + tick*59 + 503)` for jitter + draw, deterministic spine (#3); the whole phase is try/caught so the tick loop never crashes, with a fallback table for empty pool / missing template / unknown unlock id / destroyed seat / unknown reach (#4); additive throughout except the Starter-12 rework which is explicitly THR-501 (#6). UI pillar (notifications, card-flight reveal, debug tab, hex signifier) is deferred to later child issues — this is engine substrate only, no UI surface (browser-verify N/A). 11 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantBeat.test.ts`): Director offer/skip/fail-soft/spine-hold, trigger eval (turn + first_bonded via a thread edge), deterministic weighted `drawFromPool`, `resolveAscendantBeat`, `unlock_action` grow + idempotency, and trace-category registration. Verification: full suite **11660 green**, `vite build` green, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (3483 pre-existing baseline, THR-489 — all my files compile clean), and the 30-tick CLI smoke (`seed 42`, medium) reached tick 30 with 398 agents and no throw, with the Director firing spine beat `beat.spine.opening` on turn 1 (`pending` set, `spineCursor` → 1). Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-494** (2026-06-26, THR-493 G1) — Ascendant-reach test fixtures + harness identity param. Built the foundation for the Content Health Report matrix (per-seed × per-ascendant-reach trigger coverage): eight deterministic `AscendantIdentity` fixtures, one per primary Reach, plus a reusable identity-path state builder and a `--ascendant` flag on the gameplay-report harness. New `src/data/__fixtures__/ascendant-reach-fixtures.ts` declares the canonical 1:1 `REACH_PRIMARY_SPHERE` map (Force→Iron, Matter→Stone, Energy→Eye, Life→Gold, Mind→Veil, Spirit→Heart, Time→Star, Entropy→Shadow — authoritative for the fixtures; deliberately distinct from the fuzzy one-to-many affinity maps in `meetingEncounter.ts`/`agendaGenerator.ts`), the ordered `REPORT_ASCENDANT_REACHES` axis (the NFP constant), a pure `buildReachAscendantIdentity(reach, map)` (primary sphere = the reach's paired sphere; secondary sphere = the next Creation sphere cyclically, always distinct; `domainAffinities` top out at the pinned reach so it's the dominant reach), the baked `ASCENDANT_REACH_FIXTURES` record, and `getAscendantReachIdentity(reach)` which fails soft on an unknown reach with a clear error listing the valid reaches. New `src/testing/ascendantReachHarness.ts` exposes `buildAscendantReachState(reach, seed, {mapSize?, cosmology?})` over the engine's `initializeGameStateFromIdentity` (cosmology derived from the identity by default; map size honors the caller). Wired `--ascendant <reach>`, `--ascendants iron,gold`, and `--all-ascendants` into `scripts/gameplay-report.ts` (runs the identity path, expands an ascendant × seed matrix, prints the resolved `primaryReach=` per run as the acceptance check, tags JSON output with the reach) and added `resetDecisionCache()` to `runOnce` so every report run is deterministic regardless of order — this also fixed cross-run `npc_NNNN` ID drift on the existing random-archetype path. NFP: all magic numbers named (`PRIMARY_DOMAIN_AFFINITY`, `SECONDARY_DOMAIN_AFFINITY`); determinism via fixed fields + counter resets; fail-soft on unknown reach. Three pillars: Engine (fixtures + helper + harness param); Content N/A (config, not prose); UI N/A. 5 fixture/determinism tests (`src/testing/__tests__/ascendantReachFixtures.test.ts`) — all 8 fixtures × 4 seeds build valid state without throwing, same (reach, seed) → identical structural fingerprint, unknown reach throws a listing error. Full suite 11649 green; `vite build` green; zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3482 pre-existing baseline, THR-489, no errors mention the new files); acceptance run `npm run gameplay-report -- --ascendant heart --seed 42 --ticks 60` reached tick 60 and reported `primaryReach=heart`. Browser-verify N/A (no UI surface). Unblocks the rest of THR-493.

✅ **THR-476** (2026-06-23) — Find-first content-lint (THR-414 verdict #3 — "Find gates Change/Control" kept as a soft per-template convention enforced by lint, not a hard engine invariant). New advisory `scripts/lint-find-first.ts` + `npm run lint:find-first` flags `crudType:'update'` (Change/Control) `UnifiedActionTemplate`s that lack a Find gate. **Load-bearing interpretation:** there is no per-template Find-prerequisite field; the engine's "perceive before you act" gate is layer revelation (`narrativeLayer`, Gate 7 in `getTargetActionSlots`), so the lint treats **"Found" = `narrativeLayer` present** — and deliberately adds no schema field. A template passes if `narrativeLayer` is set, or `starter === true` (THR-419 starter-floor), or its id is in the curated script-local `FIND_GATE_EXEMPT_IDS` (empty by default — every entry needs a rationale). Severity is warning-only and the script **always exits 0**: it's advisory, the flagged list is the content-author worklist, and promoting it to CI-blocking is a deliberate follow-up (mirrors `lint:encounter-content`, which fails only on errors). Iterates `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` (already aggregates every encounter-content array), groups warnings by reach, prints a summary. Baseline: 226 Change/Control templates → 27 gated, 5 starter, 0 exempt, 194 flagged. Exported pure `classifyFindGate`/`runFindFirstLint` with 6 fixture tests (`scripts/__tests__/lint-find-first.test.ts`). Dev-tooling only (lint reads templates; Content/UI/Engine N/A per the verdict — not a runtime invariant): `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `vite build` green, full suite 11644 green. PR pending.
✅ **THR-488** (2026-06-23) — Branch & worktree amnesty (aggressive prune; Workstream 3 of the 2026-06-23 consolidation). Cut the git sprawl the issue cited (423 branches / 294 worktrees as of that morning) down to the active set — by pickup time the daily cleanup task and prior sessions had already cleared the worktrees (3 active: home on `thr-75`, plus two live CC worktrees), so the real work was branches: **32 → 14 local, 70 → 16 remote**. Deleted **18 local + 53 remote** branches across three buckets: (1) 27 merged-to-`main` remotes (content already shipped — unconditionally safe); (2) 8 `christianspliid/thr-*` whose Linear issue resolved **Done** (THR-107/234/284/344/345/406/451/452 — including same-day 451/452, since the issue-state gate has no age floor for the work-branch namespace); (3) abandoned process/ephemeral branches >30 days old (`pickup/thr-*`, `docs/plan-flush-*`, `codex/thr-*`, `temp/*`, and unnamed `claude/*` session branches). **Every safety rail in the issue procedure was honored:** a pre-delete SHA snapshot of all 102 refs was written to `Docs/audits/2026-06-23-thr488-branch-snapshot.txt` with restore commands (any branch recoverable via `git push origin <sha>:refs/heads/<name>`); every `christianspliid/thr-*` candidate's Linear state was verified Done before deletion (all 24 embedded-issue ids checked — all closed); all three checked-out worktree branches, `main`, and every branch <30 days old were preserved; the mutex was respected (only THR-488 itself was In Dev, so no other session's branch was touched). Kept out of scope: `feat/agent-ambition-system` (2026-03-16, not in the listed abandoned namespaces) and all <30-day session/process branches (the 30-day rail protects them — next month's run collects them). Git-housekeeping + one snapshot doc; zero `src/` changes — engine-smoke and browser-verify N/A.

✅ **THR-487** (2026-06-23) — CI cleanup, merge = Done. Deleted the dead `.github/workflows/claude-review.yml` — its review verdict was a permanent `skip` placeholder (THR-182/183 never replaced it), so its `transition-to-done` job (gated on `verdict == 'pass'`) never fired and any issue reaching In Review stalled forever. Rewrote `.github/workflows/linear-autoclose.yml`: it now transitions referenced issues straight to **Done** (resolving the team's `completed`-type state at runtime, robust to renames) instead of the dead-end In Review, and gained a `pull_request: [closed]` merged trigger that scans the PR **title + body** for `(Fixes|Closes|Resolves) THR-XX` so a PR-body keyword closes the issue even when a non-squash merge commit drops it (impediment #140); the original push-to-main commit-message scan is retained as a fallback. Both trigger paths share one idempotent transition loop (already-completed/cancelled issues skip), so the double-fire on a merged PR is harmless. `ci.yml` left untouched — still the required `Test · Typecheck · Build` gate (job name preserved → no branch-protection change). YAML + embedded github-script JS syntax-validated; 11638 tests green; vite build clean. Workflow-only change (no `src/`, no UI, no engine) — engine-smoke and browser-verify N/A. THR-182 (ARC-6) / THR-183 (ARC-10) found already Done + archived (not open as the handoff assumed), so annotated as superseded rather than force-cancelled. CLAUDE.md DoD wording left to WS1 (THR-486) per the plan's mutex note. PR pending.

Exported `ConditionalSublocationSpec` + added optional `reach?` field (derivable from the registry key, present for standalone-spec census tagging); added `getSublocationFamilyCounts()` debug read (NFP #2) for P2b desert-fill verification. Census adapter `resolveSublocations` now iterates the registry by reach (key is the authoritative reach), so P2b families are picked up automatically — output identical today (7 Gold entries, `empty:7`). Pure relocation + loop generalization, additive (NFP #6), phase signature unchanged. 11638 tests pass; vite build clean; zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (deterministic stash-diff: 3482 baseline == 3482 with changes, identical touched-file signatures, THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verify exempt: engine-only, no UI surface (Gold sublocations render unchanged via existing surfaces). Unblocks THR-469 P2b.
