✅ **THR-477** (2026-06-23) — Content Census value authoring (scale axis + condition/omen reach) **plus completion of THR-474's unshipped schema plumbing**. THR-474 was marked Done but its core deliverable — the optional `censusTag` field and `src/types/contentCensus.ts` — never reached `main` (only its plan doc did); as sole executor it would never be re-picked, so THR-477 was permanently blocked. Landed the schema (`ContentCensusTag { reach?, scale? }` on `PossessionNodeProperties`, `TraitDefinitionProperties`, `SpellTemplate`, `ArtifactTemplate`, `OmenTrackTemplate`, `ConditionalSublocationSpec`; the 7 Gold sublocation specs tagged + `resolveSublocations` de-hardcoded). **Discovered the census adapters never read `censusTag`** (they only derived reach from `effects[]` and always set scale=null) — so authored values would have been inert; wired all five registry adapters (`resolveAttachments`/`resolveSpells`/`resolveArtifacts`/`resolveConditions`/`resolveOmens`) to prefer authored `censusTag` with derivation fallback, and added `dominantReachFromContributions` (largest |magnitude|) so condition reach derives from `domainContributions`. Then authored values per `Docs/plans/2026-06-23-content-census-value-authoring-rubric.md`: `censusTag.scale` on all 177 applicable entries (6 conditions personal, 44 omens regional/cosmic, 119 attachments via subcategory→tier, 5 spells via targeting, 3 artifacts cosmic) and `censusTag.reach` on the 47 no-derivation entries (44 omens spread across all 8 reaches — veil 11 / stone 9 / eye 6 / star 6 / heart 4 / iron 4 / gold 2 / shadow 2; 7 cosmic / 37 regional; plus 2 effect-less attachments + 1 effect-less spell). Derivable reach left to the adapter (single source of truth, NFP #2/#3). Result: `npm run content-census` shows all five target registries move **0% → 100% both-resolvable** (omen reach 0→100%, scale off 0% everywhere). 11638 tests pass; vite build clean; engine smoke (tick 30, 398 agents) green; zero net-new type errors (verified by deterministic stash-diff against the pre-existing `tsc -b` baseline). Browser-verify exempt (data-only / offline census artifact, no UI surface). Landed the bouncing rubric plan doc in the same PR.

✅ **THR-491** (2026-06-23) — CI skips the heavy `Test · Typecheck · Build` suite on doc-only changes. Added a cheap `detect` job (`dorny/paths-filter@v3`) to `.github/workflows/ci.yml` that flags whether any non-doc file changed, and gated the heavy `check` job on `if: needs.detect.outputs.code == 'true'`. A required job skipped via a job-level `if:` reports `skipped`, which GitHub counts as **success** for branch protection — so doc-only PRs merge with no 20-min run, while code/mixed PRs run the full suite exactly as before. The `check` job keeps the exact name `Test · Typecheck · Build`, so the branch-protection required-context config needs no change. Skips `**/*.md`, `Docs/**`, `.planning/**`, `Design/**`; still gates `src/**`, `world-model.json`, `package.json`/lockfile, `.github/**`, and any mixed doc+code PR. Workflow-level `permissions: { contents: read, pull-requests: read }` lets paths-filter list changed files via the GitHub API. **Two-PR history:** the initial implementation (PR #385) shipped a non-functional filter — under dorny/paths-filter's default `predicate-quantifier: 'some'` (OR), the leading `'**'` pattern matched every file, so `code` was always `true` and doc-only PRs still ran the full suite. The original test PR #386 was a false positive: it predated the `detect` job on its base, so it reported no `check` job at all and merely looked CLEAN. Diagnosed via a real doc-only PR (#388) whose `detect` log showed `Filter code = true` on three `Docs/*.md` files. Fixed by adding `predicate-quantifier: 'every'` (each file must match `'**'` AND none of the negations → only non-doc files qualify), then re-verified that a doc-only PR reports `check` = skipped. PRs #385 + #388.

✅ **THR-433** (2026-05-14) — Kindle a Calling, internal-pressure divine action. New `action.faction.kindle_a_calling` (heart/force, cost 10, tier 2) replaces a faction's `pursues` ambition with one drawn by a four-signal bias resolver: (a) member axiological pulls — sum of `axiologicalProfile` across living non-army members, normalized for faction size; (b) leader bias — the seat's profile weighted 2× higher; (c) doctrine pressure — `recoveredDoctrineId` adds bias toward cultural_dominance + divine_mandate; (d) recent dissent — dissentLevel ≥ KINDLE_CALLING_DISSENT_BIAS_THRESHOLD (0.35) pulls toward defensive_consolidation and away from territorial_expansion. The bias map is added to `scoreEligibleAmbitions` weights (newly exported from `factionAmbitions.ts`, along with `selectAmbitionType`, `mulberry32`, `hashString`). KINDLE_CALLING_ESSENCE_SHARPENING (1.4) scales bias contributions. A seeded PRNG draws the chosen candidate using `state.seed + state.tick * 47 + hashString(factionId) + KINDLE_CALLING_PRNG_SALT`. The old ambition node is removed; a new `amb_${factionId}_${tick}_kindled` ambition node is added with `kindled: true` properties + a new `pursues` edge. `getArmyLockedAmbitionId` refuses to overwrite an ambition any army (`armyState != null` member) is pursuing — `lockedByArmy: true` trace branch fires instead. Plants `faction.encounter.calling_named` (2 steps, 2 aftermath reactions: commit → reputation tally + follow-on encounter seed (faction_internal_pressure, delay 5); stall → hidden mark `calling_stalled` revealable to faction_internal_pressure encounters) on the leader. Leader gets the `kindled_calling_pending` condition for KINDLED_CALLING_PENDING_DURATION (18) ticks. New `FactionKindleCallingTrace` with `previousAmbitionType`, `newAmbitionType`, `candidates` (bias-weighted), `lockedByArmy`, `noEligibleCandidates`, `seededEncounterId`, `leaderId`. 11 new constants in `faction-action-constants.ts`. New `FactionGovernanceVerbKind: 'kindle_a_calling'` dispatch added to `factionGovernanceVerbs.ts`; `unifiedActionResolution.ts` type assertion widened. `FactionNetworkAmbition.kindled?: boolean` added — `FactionSheet.tsx` Current Agenda section shows a ▲ glyph next to the ambition name + "kindled" sublabel when the active ambition was rolled by Kindle a Calling. 9 new unit tests in `factionGovernanceVerbs.test.ts` (no-faction, no-definition, no-eligible-candidates, replacement, previous-ambition-removal, army-lock refusal, determinism across identical setups, dissent biases away from territorial_expansion, doctrine biases toward cultural_dominance). Verification: 11309 tests pass; tsc clean; vite build 12.14s; 30-tick CLI smoke green (379 agents, 79 events); browser load at 1920×1080 with 0 console errors, __DEBUG state assertion confirms template+encounter+trace+constants wired. Cowork handoff comment (2026-05-14 09:27) served as plan doc — the formal plan doc was untracked locally (third flush-plan-docs bounce noted on the issue); sibling THR-432 plan used as architectural reference. Mutex with THR-400, THR-430, THR-432 (all Done before pickup) and THR-431 (still In Design — no file collision). PR pending.

✅ **THR-430** (2026-05-14) — Schism, deferred-resolution faction-split divine action. New `action.faction.schism` (star/chaos, cost 14, tier 3) plants `schismPendingResolutionTick` + `schismBaselineCohesion` on the faction; SCHISM_PENDING_DURATION_TICKS later, `phaseSchismResolution` decides reform-or-split via `splitPressure = clamp01(0.4·cohesionDrop + 0.4·spread + 0.2·dissent)` against a seeded sample. New `performFactionSplit` mints splinter actor node, partitions the lowest-rep half of `member_of` edges, copies `relates_to`, creates hostile back-edge — observationally identical to the existing `faction_splinter` aftermath. New `performFactionReform` applies reputation penalty + SCHISM_REFORM_EXPULSION_COUNT most-misaligned expulsions (clamped to leave ≥1 standing). 9 new tunable constants in `game-config.ts`. 3 new trace categories (`schism_planted` / `schism_resolved` / `faction_reformed`) with typed interfaces in `factionAction.ts`. `plant_schism` GraphOp intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` sibling to `faction_verb`. Splinter-naming table keyed by `(factionType, dominantReach)` in `faction-schism-content.ts`. UI: pending-schism banner + post-resolution reform afterimage in `FactionDetailBody` (ThreadDetailView). Debug: `window.__DEBUG.schism.list()`. 15 new tests; 11257 total green; tsc clean; vite build 8.75s; 30-tick CLI smoke green (379 agents, 79 events). Deferred to follow-ups: HexMap pending-schism signifier ring, `faction.encounter.schism_resolution` 2-branch encounter template with 3 new conditions (`schismatic_loyalty`/`reformist_survivor`/`between_doctrines`), `__DEBUG.schism.forceResolve()`. Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-thr-430-schism-faction-split.md`.

✅ **THR-12** (2026-05-14) — Hex Vignette Phase 4: Raycaster-based landmark interaction and GPU-attribute selection easing. New `LandmarkRaycaster.pickLandmark()` replaces screen-space projection with `THREE.Raycaster.intersectObjects` across all InstancedMesh batches — precise at any camera angle. New `VignetteSelectionState` tracks hover/selection per `(batchKey, instanceIndex)`, eases `aHoverMix`/`aSelectionMix` toward target via `tickEasing(deltaMs, layer)` — per-frame smooth GPU-attribute transitions without React re-renders. Shader (`VignetteInstanceMaterial`) gained `aSelectionMix` attribute + `vSelectionMix` varying + `uSelectionColor` uniform (gold tint, blended after hover). `ChunkedLandmarkLayer` gained `getBatchInfos()` (readonly view of all batches), `setInstanceAttribute()` (updateRange-scoped GPU upload), and `aSelectionMix` zero-initialization in `applyInstances`. Canvas: `pointermove` calls `pickLandmark` → `setHovered`, cursor flips to `pointer` on hit; `pointerdown` calls `pickLandmark` → `setSelected` + fires `onLandmarkSelect`; `keydown:Escape` calls `clearAll()` + `onLandmarkSelect('', '')`; render loop passes `deltaMs` to `tickEasing` each frame. Debug: `showClickTargetSpheres` toggle (in `VignetteDebugOverlay`) renders translucent fill circles at click-target world positions. `window.__TERRAIN_LAB` extended with `selectLandmark(id)`, `gotoLandmark(id)`, `getSelectionState()`, `clearSelection()`. 7 new Phase-4 constants (`HOVER_MIX_TARGET=0.45`, `SELECTION_MIX_TARGET=0.9`, `HOVER_MIX_EASE_MS=120`, `SELECTION_MIX_EASE_MS=160`, `CLICK_DEBUG_SPHERE_OPACITY=0.55`, `RAYCASTER_LANDMARK_RECURSIVE=false`, `LANDMARK_HOVER_CURSOR='pointer'`). 15 new tests (5 raycaster: miss, no-registry, hit, stale-registry, negative-instanceId; 10 selection-state: hover/select/clear, easing progression, fail-soft on stale key, reverse on clear). Verification: 11257/11257 tests pass (734 files), tsc clean, vite build 7.55s. PR pending.

✅ **THR-388** (2026-05-08) — Hexmap tooltip 1-second hover delay. Tooltip HTML overlay previously appeared immediately on mouse-over. Added debounced `tooltipHex` state to `HexMapV2.tsx` — a `useRef` timer clears/resets on every `hoveredHex` change and commits `tooltipHex` after `TOOLTIP_HOVER_DELAY_MS` (1 000 ms). Clearing the timer on hex-leave means the tooltip never appears for quick pass-through hovers. The `hoveredHex` prop and all Three.js hover highlights (ring, selection overlay, status bar) remain instant — only the HTML tooltip layer is delayed. Named constant `TOOLTIP_HOVER_DELAY_MS: 1000` added to `INTERACTION_CONSTANTS` in `src/components/HexMapV2/interaction/HexRaycaster.ts` (NFP #1 tunability). No changes to `HexTooltip.tsx`, `HexV2View.tsx`, or the tooltip's content/layout. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vite build` clean, `npm test -- --run` pass. PR #230.

✅ **THR-139** (2026-05-08) — Authored `intel_referenced_prose` aftermath reaction variant (Encounter Format Migration). Closes the intelligence consumption loop's authored "intel paid off" surface — fourth and most player-visible of the five consumption sites (after THR-113 scoring/prose/resolution and THR-140 difficulty modifier). New union variant `kind: 'intel_referenced_prose'` on `EncounterAftermathReactionEffect` (`src/types/unifiedAction.ts`) with `category` + 3-band `prose: { reliable, uncertain?, dubious? }` + optional `significance` / `targetAgentId` / `when`. Engine: dispatcher case in `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` (`src/engine/encounterAftermath.ts`) reads the agent's intelligence records, picks the most-recent matching record by category + action context (locationId / region / templateId via `TEMPLATE_CATEGORY_MATCHERS`), selects the prose variant for the matched record's reliability band (with upward inheritance: dubious → uncertain → reliable), and appends a `narrative` `TickEvent` to `recentEvents` / `tickEvents`. Two new helpers exported from `src/engine/intelligence.ts`: `findIntelReferencedProseMatch` and `pickIntelReferencedProseLine`. `emitIntelligenceReferenced` widened with `referencedBy: 'aftermath_prose'` discriminator (matched widening in `src/types/trace.ts`). Fail-soft with 4 skip-path traces (`no_target_agent` / `no_matching_record` / `skipped_dubious` / `skipped_empty_prose`); records are read, never consumed. Four new tunables in `src/data/agent-behavior-constants.ts`: `INTEL_REFERENCED_PROSE_SIGNIFICANCE_RELIABLE` (0.6), `_UNCERTAIN` (0.45), `_DUBIOUS` (0.3), and master switch `INTEL_REFERENCED_PROSE_DUBIOUS_FIRES` (true). Content: new `src/data/intelligence-referenced-prose.ts` ships the 72-line shared prose pack (6 categories × 3 bands × 4 lines); voice contract is Threadbare past-tense third-person, 18–32 words/line, sphere-tinted, dubious band explicitly hedges or shows the intel betraying the agent. 3 pilot reactions wired: `arcane-circle-encounter-content.ts` (`agent_network` — Enchantment commission), `builders-fellowship-encounter-content.ts` (`political_secret` — Hidden backing failure), `encounter-anomaly-content.ts` (`cultural_knowledge` — Amber-sap grove). Content invariant whitelist updated (`src/testing/contentInvariants.ts`). UI pillar N/A by design — the new `TickEvent.type: 'narrative'` flows through the existing chronicle/right-rail renderer (browser-verified on `?view=game&seeded` at 1920×1080: 3 narrative TickEvent toasts active in DOM, confirming the shared rendering surface is live; my new effect produces the identical `TickEvent` shape). Wiring docs: `Docs/plans/wiring-checklist.md` consumption-hooks table extended with the `aftermath_prose` row (5 hooks total) plus an authoring paragraph; `Docs/plans/2026-04-16-systemic-wiring-guide.md` Capability 6 widened from "four places" to "five places" with full description and authoring guidance, Aftermath Reaction Effect Types table count bumped 19 → 20 with `intel_referenced_prose` row added. Tests: 18 assertions across 3 new files — `src/engine/__tests__/intelligence.referencedProse.test.ts` (9: targetEntityId/region/templateId match, wrong-category, wrong-agent, most-recent, band selection with inheritance, empty-string), `src/engine/__tests__/encounterAftermath.intelReferencedProse.test.ts` (7: success path, no-record skip, trace shape, dubious-band selection, upward inheritance, empty-prose skip, significance override), `src/engine/__tests__/contracts/intel-aftermath-prose-liveness.contract.test.ts` (2: end-to-end success liveness, no-record fail-soft). NFP audit clean: tunability (4 named constants), inspectability (1 trace + 5 dispatcher traces), determinism (no PRNG), fail-soft (4 skip paths + try/catch), narrative-over-mechanical (the whole point), additive (no signature changes), performance (one category-filtered scan per `intel_referenced_prose` effect — aftermath off the per-tick critical path). Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npx vite build` clean (7.23s), `npm test -- --run` 723 files / 11068 tests pass (43.37s). Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-08-thr-139-intel-referenced-prose-reaction.md` (still pending `flush-plan-docs` commit at landing time). Four follow-up Deferral issues filed per plan §Deferrals: template-walk content sweep, per-reaction dedup, `{intel:category.acquiredDaysAgo}` placeholder, lint rule for category mismatch.

Three Connective Pieces: (1) GraphOp Executor — src/types/graphOp.ts (6 op types, symbolic $refs, resolveRef) + src/engine/graphOpExecutor.ts (fail-soft batch execution, graph_op_execution traces), (2) Template Enrichment — src/data/action-template-content.ts (36 templates: 4 per reach × 9 reaches, onSuccess/onFailure GraphOps, difficulty 0.1-0.7, narrativeTemplates), (3) ActionInProgress Lifecycle — src/engine/actionLifecycle.ts (create/progress/complete with performing edges) + src/engine/actionCandidates.ts (location subtype + actor affinity filtering). Orchestrator phaseActionProgress resolves completed actions via GraphOp executor (success = rng < 1-difficulty). DebugPanel ActionExecutionDetail renderer. Integration test: 6 tests covering full lifecycle, all 36 templates, success/failure GraphOps. 74 CRUD tests across 8 test files. Design doc + 10-task plan in Docs/plans/

- Road-Aware Agent Movement: ✅ Complete (2026-03-25) — Full mechanical integration of roads and trails into gameplay, implemented per design doc (6 decisions). (1) Road-aware pathfinding: Dijkstra considers road edges (both outgoing and incoming) with discount multipliers (major 0.4×, trail 0.7×), PathResult extended with RoadSegmentInfo[]. (2) Hex-level movement state: MovementState gains currentHexPosition, roadHexQueue, roadHexCost, currentRoadType (all optional, backward-compat). (3) Road mode in tickMovement: agents advance hex-by-hex along road hexPath, located_at only updates on arrival at location nodes, mixed path support. (4) Gated re-evaluation: moving agents get 5-guard decision check (tick gating, score comparison, 1.5× reroute threshold, action-type guard, target invalidation) instead of blanket skip. (5) Animation road mode: startRoadHopAnimation factory (300ms major / 500ms trail), reduced wobble (0.3×), hop chaining without settle bounce until final hex. (6) Trace types: RoadHexTransitionTrace and AgentRerouteTrace. Design doc: Docs/plans/2026-03-25-road-aware-movement-design.md. 5,896 tests pass.
